types = {
	steal_small_arms_INDO_COMMIE = {
		category = insurgency_raids_state
		days_to_prepare = 14
		custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_army_size = { size < 3 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				tag = VIE
				has_war = yes
				surrender_progress > 0
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				VIN_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FRE_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		command_power = 1
		allowed = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			OR = {
				tag = VIN
				tag = VIE
				tag = LOS
				tag = NLF
				tag = MEO
				tag = NUN
				tag = FUL
				tag = TAI
				tag = TAM
				tag = THO
				tag = PQC
			}
		}
		visible = {
			is_in_array = {
				FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = ROOT.id
			}
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 8
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
		}
		available = {
			VIN = {
				has_country_flag = Arsenal_Raids_Unlocked
			}	
			
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		launchable = {
		}
		show_target = {
			INDO_raid_not_own_side_trigger = yes	# never a friendly, a subject or a co-belligerent
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					tag = FRE
				}
				FROM = {
					tag = CCC
				}
				FROM = {
					tag = VIE
				}
			}
		}
		arrow = {
			type = line
		}
		essential_equipment = {
			#infantry_equipment = 50
		}
		additional_equipment = {
		}
		target_type = {
			building = {
				type = arms_factory
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				infantry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				militia = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				marine = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				mechanized_marine = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				mountaineers = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				airborne = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				mechanized_airborne = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				motorized_infantry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				mechanized_infantry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				armored_infantry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				ffl = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				air_cavalry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		starting_point = {
			types = {
				air_base
				naval_base
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 30
						str_damage = 10
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_award_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 10 }
					indochina_raid_deescalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 10
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 20
						str_damage = 5
					}
					var:actor_country = {
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = infantry_equipment
							amount = 100
							producer = FRA
						}
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 5 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 100
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_limited_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 25
						str_damage = 2
					}
					var:actor_country = {
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = infantry_equipment
							amount = 200
							producer = FRA
						}
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.2
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 6 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 10 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 250
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 20
						str_damage = 0
					}
					var:actor_country = {
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = infantry_equipment
							amount = 500
							producer = FRA
						}
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = support_equipment
							amount = 100
							producer = FRA
						}
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.35
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 15 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 25 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 500
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_critical_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			disaster = {
				base = 0.05
			}
			success = {
				base = 0.4
				experience = {
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 0.5
				}
				organisation = {
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 100
				}
				strength = {
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 1
				}
				recon = {
					weight = 0.08
					reference = 10
				}
				### Target Modifiers
				resistance = {
					reference = 100
					start_reference = 20					# Resistance below 20 doesn't help
					weight = 0.4
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.25
					start_weight = -0.25
				}
				anti_air = {
					reference = 0
					weight = 0
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 0
					weight = 0
				}
				enemy_units = {
					reference = 2					# More than 2 DIVs in the target Province shouldn't make any difference
					weight = -0.5
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.2
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}
			critical = {
				base = 0.15
			}
		}
	}

		custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_army_size = { size < 3 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				tag = VIE
				has_war = yes
				surrender_progress > 0
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				VIN_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FRE_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		command_power = 1
		allowed = {
			OR = {
				tag = VIN
				tag = VIE
				tag = LOS
				tag = NLF
				tag = MEO
				tag = NUN
				tag = FUL
				tag = TAI
				tag = TAM
				tag = THO
				tag = PQC
			}
		}
		visible = {
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 7
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
		}
		available = {
			is_in_array = {
				FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = ROOT.id
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		launchable = {
		}
		show_target = {
			INDO_raid_not_own_side_trigger = yes	# never a friendly, a subject or a co-belligerent
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					tag = FRE
				}
				FROM = {
					tag = CCC
				}
				FROM = {
					tag = VIE
				}
			}
		}
		arrow = {
			type = line
		}
		essential_equipment = {
			#infantry_equipment = 50
		}
		additional_equipment = {
		}
		target_type = {
			state = {
				arms_factory > 0
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				infantry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				militia = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				marine = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {

				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {

			}

		unit_requirements = {

			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				mechanized_airborne = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				motorized_infantry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				mechanized_infantry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				armored_infantry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {

					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				air_cavalry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		starting_point = {
			types = {
				air_base
				naval_base
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 30
						str_damage = 10
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_award_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 10 }
					indochina_raid_deescalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 10
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 20
						str_damage = 5
					}
					var:actor_country = {
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = artillery_equipment
							amount = 25
							producer = FRA
						}
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_penalise_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 5 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 100
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_limited_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 25
						str_damage = 2
					}
					var:actor_country = {
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = artillery_equipment
							amount = 50
							producer = FRA
						}
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.2
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 6 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_penalise_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 10 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 250
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 20
						str_damage = 0
					}
					var:actor_country = {
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = artillery_equipment
							amount = 75
							producer = FRA
						}
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = motorized_equipment
							amount = 25
							producer = FRA
						}
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.35
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 15 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 6 }
					indochina_raid_penalise_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 25 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 500
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_critical_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			disaster = {
				base = 0.07
			}
			success = {
				base = 0.3
				experience = {
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 0.5
				}
				organisation = {
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 100
				}
				strength = {
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 1
				}
				recon = {
					weight = 0.08
					reference = 10
				}
				### Target Modifiers
				resistance = {
					reference = 100
					start_reference = 20					# Resistance below 20 doesn't help
					weight = 0.4
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.25
					start_weight = -0.25
				}
				anti_air = {
					reference = 0
					weight = 0
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 0
					weight = 0
				}
				enemy_units = {
					reference = 2					# More than 2 DIVs in the target Province shouldn't make any difference
					weight = -0.5
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.2
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}
			critical = {
				base = 0.1
			}
		}
	}

		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_army_size = { size < 3 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				tag = VIE
				has_war = yes
				surrender_progress > 0
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				VIN_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FRE_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		command_power = 1
		allowed = {
			OR = {
				tag = VIN
				tag = VIE
				tag = LOS
				tag = NLF
				tag = MEO
				tag = NUN
				tag = FUL
				tag = TAI
				tag = TAM
				tag = THO
				tag = PQC
			}
		}
		visible = {
			is_in_array = {
				FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = ROOT.id
			}
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 6
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
		}
		available = {
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		launchable = {
		}
		show_target = {
			INDO_raid_not_own_side_trigger = yes	# never a friendly, a subject or a co-belligerent
			FROM = {
				tag = VIN
			}
		}
		arrow = {
			type = line
		}
		essential_equipment = {
			#infantry_equipment = 50
		}
		additional_equipment = {
		}
		target_type = {
			state = {
				arms_factory > 0
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				infantry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {

					min = 2
				}

				naval_base

		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 30
						str_damage = 10
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_award_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 10 }
					indochina_raid_deescalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 10
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 20
						str_damage = 5
					}

						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = mechanized_equipment
							amount = 50
							producer = FRA
						}
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_penalise_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 5 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 100
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_limited_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 25
						str_damage = 2
					}
					var:actor_country = {
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = mechanized_equipment
							amount = 100
							producer = FRA
						}
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.2
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 6 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_penalise_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 10 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 250
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 20
						str_damage = 0
					}
					var:actor_country = {
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = mechanized_equipment
							amount = 150
							producer = FRA
						}
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = sp_artillery_equipment
							amount = 25
							producer = FRA
						}
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.35
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 15 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 6 }
					indochina_raid_penalise_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 25 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 500
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_critical_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			disaster = {
				base = 0.1
			}
			success = {
				base = 0.2
				experience = {
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 0.5
				}
				organisation = {
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 100
				}
				strength = {
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 1
				}
				recon = {
					weight = 0.08
					reference = 10
				}
				### Target Modifiers
				resistance = {
					reference = 100
					start_reference = 20					# Resistance below 20 doesn't help
					weight = 0.4
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.25
					start_weight = -0.25
				}
				anti_air = {
					reference = 0
					weight = 0
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 0
					weight = 0
				}
				enemy_units = {
					reference = 2					# More than 2 DIVs in the target Province shouldn't make any difference
					weight = -0.5
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.2
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}
			critical = {
				base = 0.05
			}
		}

		category = insurgency_raids_state
		days_to_prepare = 14
		custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_army_size = { size < 3 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				tag = VIE
				has_war = yes
				surrender_progress > 0
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				VIN_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FRE_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		command_power = 1
		allowed = {
		}
		visible = {
			OR = {
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = ROOT.id
				}
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = ROOT.id
				}
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = ROOT.id
				}
			}
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 6
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
		}
		available = {
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		launchable = {
		}
		show_target = {
			INDO_raid_not_own_side_trigger = yes	# never a friendly, a subject or a co-belligerent
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id
					}
				}
			}
			var:target_state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					AND = {
					is_core_of = CAM
						NOT = { ROOT = { tag = VIN } }
					}
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}
		arrow = {
			type = line
		}
		essential_equipment = {
			#infantry_equipment = 50
		}
		additional_equipment = {
		}
		target_type = {
			building = {
				type = bunker
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				infantry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				militia = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				marine = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				mechanized_marine = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				mountaineers = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				airborne = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				mechanized_airborne = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				motorized_infantry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				mechanized_infantry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				armored_infantry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				ffl = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = {
				air_cavalry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		starting_point = {
			types = {
				air_base
				naval_base
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						damage_building = {
							type = bunker
							damage = 0.05							# levels of damage
							province = var:ROOT.target_province
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 30
						str_damage = 10
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_award_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 10 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 10
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						damage_building = {
							type = bunker
							damage = 0.15							# levels of damage
							province = var:ROOT.target_province
							repair_speed_modifier = -0.05
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 20
						str_damage = 5
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 5 }
					indochina_raid_deescalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 100
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_limited_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						damage_building = {
							type = bunker
							damage = 0.85							# levels of damage
							province = var:ROOT.target_province
							repair_speed_modifier = -0.5
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 25
						str_damage = 2
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.2
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 6 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 10 }
					indochina_raid_deescalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 250
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 20
						str_damage = 0
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.35
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 15 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 25 }
					indochina_raid_deescalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 500
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_critical_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			disaster = {
				base = 0.075
			}
			success = {
				base = 0.25
				experience = {
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 0.5
				}
				organisation = {
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 100
				}
				strength = {
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 1
				}
				recon = {
					weight = 0.08
					reference = 10
				}
				### Target Modifiers
				resistance = {
					reference = 100
					start_reference = 20					# Resistance below 20 doesn't help
					weight = 0.4
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.25
					start_weight = -0.25
				}
				anti_air = {
					reference = 0
					weight = 0
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 0
					weight = 0
				}
				enemy_units = {
					reference = 2					# More than 2 DIVs in the target Province shouldn't make any difference
					weight = -0.5
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.2
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}
			critical = {
				base = 0.05
			}
		}
	}

				air_cavalry = {
					min = 2
				}
			}
		}
		starting_point = {
			types = {
				air_base
				naval_base
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						damage_building = {
							type = bunker
							damage = 0.05							# levels of damage
							province = var:ROOT.target_province
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 30
						str_damage = 10
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_award_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 10 }
					indochina_raid_deescalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 10
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						damage_building = {
							type = bunker
							damage = 0.15							# levels of damage
							province = var:ROOT.target_province
							repair_speed_modifier = -0.05
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 20
						str_damage = 5
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_penalise_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 5 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 100
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_limited_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						damage_building = {
							type = bunker
							damage = 0.85							# levels of damage
							province = var:ROOT.target_province
							repair_speed_modifier = -0.5
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 25
						str_damage = 2
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.2
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 6 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_penalise_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 10 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 250
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 20
						str_damage = 0
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.35
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 15 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 6 }
					indochina_raid_penalise_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 25 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 500
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_critical_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
		}

			disaster = {
				base = 0.075
			}
			success = {
				base = 0.25
				experience = {
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 0.5
				}
				organisation = {
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 100
				}
				strength = {
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 1
				}
				recon = {
					weight = 0.08
					reference = 10
				}
				### Target Modifiers
				resistance = {
					reference = 100

					weight = 0.4
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.25

				}

					reference = 0
					weight = 0

					reference = 0
					weight = 0

					weight = 0.2
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}
			critical = {
				base = 0.05

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_army_size = { size < 3 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				tag = VIE
				has_war = yes
				surrender_progress > 0
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				VIN_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FRE_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		command_power = 1
		allowed = {
		}
		visible = {
			OR = {
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = ROOT.id
				}
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = ROOT.id
				}
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = ROOT.id
				}
			}
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 6
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
		}
		available = {
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		launchable = {
		}
		show_target = {
			INDO_raid_not_own_side_trigger = yes	# never a friendly, a subject or a co-belligerent
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id
					}
				}
			}
			var:target_state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					AND = {
					is_core_of = CAM
						NOT = { ROOT = { tag = VIN } }
					}
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}

			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = 0.65
				experience = {
					weight = 0.2
					start_weight = -0.2
					reference = 1
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 100					# As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
					weight = 0.1					# At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
					start_weight = -0.1					# The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
				}
				reliability = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				air_defence = {
					reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05

					reference = 1
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.1

				### Target Modifiers
				anti_air = {
					reference = 5
					weight = -0.25
				}

					reference = 500					# More than 500 fighters won't really make any difference
					weight = -0.2

					weight = 0.3
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}
			critical = {
				base = 0.15
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						damage_units = {
							province = var:ROOT.target_province
							damage = 0.05
							org_damage = 0.05
							str_damage = 0.05
							ratio = yes
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.4
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_award_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 10 }
					indochina_raid_deescalate = yes
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						damage_units = {
							province = var:ROOT.target_province
							damage = 0.1
							org_damage = 0.1
							str_damage = 0.1
							ratio = yes
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.05
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes

					indochina_raid_penalise_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 5 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_limited_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						damage_units = {
							province = var:ROOT.target_province
							damage = 0.2
							org_damage = 0.2
							str_damage = 0.2
							ratio = yes
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {

					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 6 }

					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }

					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 10 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}

							damage = 0.3
							org_damage = 0.3
							str_damage = 0.3

				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = ROOT.id
				}
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = ROOT.id
				}
			}
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
		}
		show_target = {
			INDO_raid_not_own_side_trigger = yes	# never a friendly, a subject or a co-belligerent
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id
					}
				}
			}
			var:target_state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					AND = {
					is_core_of = CAM
						NOT = { ROOT = { tag = VIN } }
					}
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}

			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}

							str_damage = 0.15

	barrel_bombing_raid_anti_commie = {
		days_to_prepare = 20
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 2
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				VIN_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FRE_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		category = insurgency_raids
		command_power = 5
		allowed = {
		}
		visible = {
			OR = {
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = ROOT.id
				}
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = ROOT.id
				}
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = ROOT.id
				}
			}
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
		}
		show_target = {
			INDO_raid_not_own_side_trigger = yes	# never a friendly, a subject or a co-belligerent
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id
					}
				}
			}
			var:target_state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					AND = {
					is_core_of = CAM
						NOT = { ROOT = { tag = VIN } }
					}
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}
		# Can prepare
		available = {
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		launchable = {
		}
		target_type = {
			state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					is_core_of = CAM
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}
		custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon
		arrow = {
			type = air
		}
		#unit_model = {
		#    type = equipment
		#}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = {
					air_transport
				}
				amount = {
					min = 24
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = {
					cas
				}
				amount = {
					min = 24
				}
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = {
					fighter
				}
				amount = {
					min = 24
				}
			}
		}
		starting_point = {
			types = {
				air_base
			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = 0.5
				experience = {
					weight = 0.2
					start_weight = -0.2
					reference = 1
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 100					# As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
					weight = 0.1					# At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
					start_weight = -0.1					# The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
				}
				reliability = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				air_defence = {
					reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				### Target Modifiers
				anti_air = {
					reference = 5
					weight = -0.25
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 500					# More than 500 fighters won't really make any difference
					weight = -0.2
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.3
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}
			critical = {
				base = 0.1
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.4
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_award_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 10 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						set_variable = {
							civilian_casualties = state_population
						}
						multiply_variable = {
							civilian_casualties = -0.001
						}
						add_manpower = civilian_casualties
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.05
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 3 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 5 }
					indochina_raid_deescalate = yes
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_limited_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						set_variable = {
							civilian_casualties = state_population
						}
						multiply_variable = {
							civilian_casualties = -0.002
						}
						add_manpower = civilian_casualties
						random_list = {
							33 = {
								damage_building = {
									type = industrial_complex
									damage = 1
								}
							}
							33 = {
								damage_building = {
									type = agri_industrial_complex
									damage = 1
								}
							}
							33 = {
								damage_building = {
									type = arms_factory
									damage = 1
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.2
					set_temp_variable = { ic_award = 6 }
					indochina_raid_award_actor = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 10 }
					indochina_raid_deescalate = yes
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						set_variable = {
							civilian_casualties = state_population
						}
						multiply_variable = {
							civilian_casualties = -0.004
						}
						add_manpower = civilian_casualties
						damage_building = {
							type = industrial_complex
							damage = 1
						}
						damage_building = {
							type = agri_industrial_complex
							damage = 1
						}
						damage_building = {
							type = arms_factory
							damage = 1

					}

						}
						add_manpower = civilian_casualties
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {

					indochina_raid_penalise_victim = yes
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 5 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}

		days_to_prepare = 20
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 2
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				VIN_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FRE_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		category = insurgency_raids
		command_power = 5
		allowed = {
		}
		visible = {
			OR = {
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = ROOT.id
				}
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = ROOT.id
				}
				is_in_array = {
					FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = ROOT.id
				}
			}
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
		}
		show_target = {
			INDO_raid_not_own_side_trigger = yes	# never a friendly, a subject or a co-belligerent
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id
					}
				}
			}
			var:target_state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					AND = {
					is_core_of = CAM
						NOT = { ROOT = { tag = VIN } }
					}
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}
		# Can prepare
		available = {
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		launchable = {
		}
		target_type = {
			state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					is_core_of = CAM
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}
		custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon
		arrow = {
			type = air
		}
		#unit_model = {
		#    type = equipment
		#}

				type = {
					tactical_bomber

					min = 24
				}
			}
		}

				air_base
			}
		}

			success = {
				base = 0.65

					weight = 0.2
					start_weight = -0.2
					reference = 1
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 100					# As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
					weight = 0.1					# At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
					start_weight = -0.1					# The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
				}

					reference = 1

					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				air_defence = {

				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5

				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id
					}
				}
			}
			var:target_state = {
				has_border_war = yes
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					AND = {
					is_core_of = CAM
						NOT = { ROOT = { tag = VIN } }
					}
					is_core_of = LOS

				base = 0.65
				experience = {
					weight = 0.2
					start_weight = -0.2
					reference = 1
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 100					# As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
					weight = 0.1					# At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
					start_weight = -0.1					# The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
				}
				reliability = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				air_defence = {
					reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.1

				victim_effects = {

				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						ROOT = {
							set_variable = {
								Chemical_Damage = 0.1
							}
							PREV = {
								add_province_modifier = {
									static_modifiers = {
										white_phosphorus
									}
									days = 60
									province = {
										all_provinces = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								owner = {
									check_variable = {
										attacker_tag = THIS
									}
									check_variable = {
										attacker_state = PREV
									}
								}
							}
							damage_units = {
								state = target_state
								damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								ratio = yes
							}
							set_border_war_data = {
								attacker = Target_State
								defender = defender_state_test
								defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
							}
						}
						else = {
							set_temp_variable = {
								defender_state_test = THIS
							}
							every_neighbor_state = {
								limit = {
									set_temp_variable = {
										attacker_state_test = THIS
									}
									has_border_war_between = {
										attacker = attacker_state_test
										defender = defender_state_test
									}
								}
								set_temp_variable = {
									Target_State = THIS
								}
								damage_units = {
									state = target_state
									damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									ratio = yes
								}
								set_border_war_data = {
									attacker = Target_State
									defender = defender_state_test
									attacker_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.05
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 5 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_limited_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}

	napalm_raid_commie = {
		days_to_prepare = 20
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 2
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				VIN_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes
			}

				factor = 0
				FRE_ai_raids_suspended_trigger = yes

		category = insurgency_raids

				FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = ROOT.id

				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
		}
		show_target = {
			INDO_raid_not_own_side_trigger = yes	# never a friendly, a subject or a co-belligerent
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = FROM.id
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id
					}
				}
			}
			var:target_state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					AND = {
					is_core_of = CAM
						NOT = { ROOT = { tag = VIN } }
					}
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}
		# Can prepare
		available = {
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
			}
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}

		}
		target_type = {
			state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN

					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}

		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon
		arrow = {
			type = air
		}
		#unit_model = {
		#    type = equipment
		#}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = {
					tactical_bomber
				}
				amount = {
					min = 24
				}
			}
		}
		starting_point = {
			types = {
				air_base
			}
		}
		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = 0.65
				experience = {
					weight = 0.2
					start_weight = -0.2
					reference = 1
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 100					# As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
					weight = 0.1					# At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
					start_weight = -0.1					# The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
				}
				reliability = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				air_defence = {
					reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				### Target Modifiers
				anti_air = {
					reference = 5
					weight = -0.25
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 500					#
					weight = -0.2
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.3
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}
			critical = {
				base = 0.15
			}
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.4
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						ROOT = {
							set_variable = {
								Chemical_Damage = 0.3
							}
							set_variable = {
								ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.001
							}
							PREV = {
								add_province_modifier = {
									static_modifiers = {
										napalm
									}
									days = 60
									province = {
										all_provinces = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								owner = {
									check_variable = {
										attacker_tag = THIS
									}
									check_variable = {
										attacker_state = PREV
									}
								}
							}
							damage_units = {
								state = target_state
								damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								ratio = yes
							}
							set_border_war_data = {
								attacker = Target_State
								defender = defender_state_test
								defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
							}
						}
						else = {
							set_temp_variable = {
								defender_state_test = THIS
							}
							every_neighbor_state = {
								limit = {
									set_temp_variable = {
										attacker_state_test = THIS
									}
									has_border_war_between = {
										attacker = attacker_state_test
										defender = defender_state_test
									}
								}
								set_temp_variable = {
									Target_State = THIS
								}
								damage_units = {
									state = target_state
									damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									ratio = yes
								}
								set_border_war_data = {
									attacker = Target_State
									defender = defender_state_test
									attacker_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								}
							}
						}
						set_variable = {
							civilian_casualties = state_population
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.002
						}
						multiply_variable = {
							civilian_casualties = ROOT.Civilian_Modifier
						}
						add_manpower = civilian_casualties
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.05
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 5 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_limited_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
						ROOT = {
							set_variable = {
								Chemical_Damage = 0.3
							}

									days = 60
									province = {
										all_provinces = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								owner = {
									check_variable = {
										attacker_tag = THIS
									}
									check_variable = {
										attacker_state = PREV
									}
								}
							}
							damage_units = {
								state = target_state
								damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								ratio = yes
							}
							set_border_war_data = {
								attacker = Target_State
								defender = defender_state_test
								defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
							}
						}
						else = {
							set_temp_variable = {
								defender_state_test = THIS
							}
							every_neighbor_state = {
								limit = {
									set_temp_variable = {
										attacker_state_test = THIS
									}
									has_border_war_between = {
										attacker = attacker_state_test
										defender = defender_state_test
									}
								}
								set_temp_variable = {
									Target_State = THIS
								}
								damage_units = {
									state = target_state
									damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
									ratio = yes
								}
								set_border_war_data = {
									attacker = Target_State
									defender = defender_state_test
									attacker_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
								}
							}
						}
						set_variable = {
							civilian_casualties = state_population
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.006
						}
						multiply_variable = {
							civilian_casualties = ROOT.Civilian_Modifier
						}
						add_manpower = civilian_casualties
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.35
					set_temp_variable = { ic_phase = 25 }
					indochina_raid_escalate = yes
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_critical_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
		}

				base = 0.65
				experience = {
					weight = 0.2
					start_weight = -0.2
					reference = 1
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 100					# As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
					weight = 0.1					# At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
					start_weight = -0.1					# The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
				}

					reference = 1
					weight = 0.05

				}

					reference = 100
					weight = 0.05

				victim_effects = {

						ROOT = {
							set_variable = {
								Chemical_Damage = 0.15
							}
							PREV = {
								add_province_modifier = {
									static_modifiers = {
										mustard_gas
									}
									days = 60
									province = {
										all_provinces = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								owner = {
									check_variable = {
										attacker_tag = THIS
									}
									check_variable = {
										attacker_state = PREV
									}
								}
							}
							set_border_war_data = {
								attacker = Target_State
								defender = defender_state_test
								defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
							}
						}
						else = {
							set_temp_variable = {
								defender_state_test = THIS
							}
							every_neighbor_state = {
								limit = {
									set_temp_variable = {
										attacker_state_test = THIS
									}
									has_border_war_between = {
										attacker = attacker_state_test
										defender = defender_state_test
									}
								}
